--- d90 [the Table] ---
The ring of pale light. The too-many-angled faces. The trillions of eyes. The ring waits. Seven doors — some open, some priced beyond you. The choice is which risk you take. ◈ the Feared — 27 distinct monsters faced blow-by-blow. The footage exists, and it plays unasked. (standing: 100) ◇ the Indispensable — The Indispensable needs a full seat — the galaxy must actually need you, not just notice you. ◈ the Beloved — Viewership 27, fan favor 37 — the galaxy's favorite, by the numbers. (standing: 100) ◇ the Witness — You didn't keep the list (0 exposures on record — the trial needs 3). ◈ the Assimilated — Always open. The table takes anyone — that's what makes it a trap, and what makes it safe. (standing: 50) ◈ the Defiant — Defiance 6 on the record. The 'no' is earned. (standing: 44) ◈ the Relay — Stage-3 neural integration. The matrix doesn't care about your kingdom — it cares how deep you are. (sysbond 0) (standing: 40)
